# Clean-up inventory

Date: 23 November 2026. Authority: the reviewer brief supplied on this date replaces earlier build instructions. No application or database changes precede this inventory.

Baseline commit: `326b9e34cd6bace97910f53bbed85b3d1af74326`. Tracked files: 229. Enumerated file, route, endpoint, component, table and literal-copy candidates: 3817. Every entry has a decision and an owner batch.

The file decision applies to all contents, including copy candidates that are code values rather than displayed prose. Tables are retained until forward migrations safely transfer permissions and account ownership. No hosted rows are deleted in the removal batch.

## Runtime findings

Live browser evidence is recorded in `artifacts/review/batch-0/crawl.json` and its screenshots. `/neon/status` is deliberately not called: its GET performs storage writes. Runtime imports currently have access to migration credentials; remove that path and retain data loading only as an operator command.
